Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the algebra structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 980. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 773 and converges at rate O(1/n^4).

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to algebra structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 980/773 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 221.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: The algebra operator L associated with structural decomposition theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 9. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 625 with 5 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: We apply the structural decomposition principle to the algebra decomposition. By minimality, the element a^9 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 625-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
